## ⏳ Megablock 6: Chrono-Mechanics & Multiverse (Items 5001-6000)

### *Time Travel, Paradox Engines & Reality Branching*

5001. [ ] Causality-tracking Paradox Engine for tactical fleet time-loops.

5002. [ ] Variable-physics dimensional breaches with dynamic constant shifts.
5003. [ ] "Mirror Verse" synchronization for encounters with alternate selves.
5004. [ ] Evolution "Fast-Forward" for observing 1M years of planetary history.
5005. [ ] Quantum Archaeology for reconstructing dead worlds from past light.
5006. [ ] Closed Timelike Curves for persistent player-driven time-travel.
5007. [ ] Dimensional siphoning mechanics causing system instability events.
5008. [ ] Entropy Reversal technology as the ultimate end-game goal.
5009. [ ] Multiverse Branching UI for managing parallel reality save-states.
5010. [ ] Reality-anchoring pylons for stabilizing multiverse collisions.
5011. [ ] Implement "Chronon Particle" harvesting from the event horizons of black holes.
5012. [ ] Add support for "Temporal Save-States" that allow players to jump between timelines.
5013. [ ] Implement a "Causality Visualization" tool for tracking the butterfly effects of actions.
5014. [ ] Create a "Multiverse Discovery Map" for identifying stable reality branches.
5015. [ ] Implement "Time-Lost Wreckage" encounters where ships from the future appear.
5016. [ ] Add support for "Temporal Paradox Warnings" that trigger reality-collapse events.
5017. [ ] Implement a "Mirror-Faction Diplomacy" engine for negotiating with alternate-selves.
5018. [ ] Develop a "Quantum Archaeology Scanner" for viewing 3D playbacks of past events.
5019. [ ] Implement "Entropy Siphoning" for powering end-game Dyson Spheres.

5021. [ ] Implement "Cross-Timeline Resource Trading" via dimensional portals.
5022. [ ] Add support for "Variable Physical Constants" (e.g., higher gravity, faster light) in breaches.
5023. [ ] Implement "Chrono-Mapping" for charting the historical evolution of the galaxy.

5025. [ ] Implement "Evolutionary Fast-Forward" mini-games for rapid planet-terraforming.

### *Self-Modifying Logic, Recursive Reality & The Omega Point*
